\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{0}}{dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\Bigg[\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\dfrac{E^{2}_{\chi}}{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}\Big\{\Big[G_{\alpha}(A,\bm{q})\big(\frac{E_{p}}{m}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{|\bm{k}|^{2}}{mE_{\chi}}\big)+G_{\delta}(A,\bm{q})(\frac{E_{p}}{m}-1)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]^{2}

(86)

\displaystyle+\frac{|\bm{k}|^{2}}{m^{2}}\Big[G_{\beta}(A,\bm{q})\frac{E_{p}-m}{E_{\chi}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+G_{\gamma}(A,\bm{q})\big(1+\frac{E_{p}}{E_{\chi}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{m}{E_{\chi}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\big)\Big]^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\dfrac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}\Big[G_{\alpha}(A,\bm{q})(\frac{E_{p}}{m}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})-G_{\delta}(A,\bm{q})(\frac{E_{p}}{m}-1)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]^{2}+

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}\Phi^{+}_{\alpha^{2}+3\delta^{2}}(\bm{q})+2\frac{|\bm{k}|^{2}}{s}\Phi^{+}_{(\alpha-\delta)^{2}+(\beta-\gamma)^{2}}(\bm{q})\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Phi^{+}_{(\alpha-\delta)^{2}+(\beta-\gamma)^{2}}(\bm{q})\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Phi^{+}_{2(\alpha\delta+\beta\gamma)}(\bm{q})

\displaystyle+2\dfrac{m^{2}}{s}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}[\Phi^{+}_{(\beta-\gamma)\beta}(\bm{q})+(1+\frac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{m^{2}})\Phi^{+}_{(\delta-\alpha)\delta}(\bm{q})]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{s}[\Phi^{+}_{(\beta-\gamma)^{2}}(\bm{q})(\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}+\cos^{4}\frac{\theta}{2})+\Phi^{+}_{(\gamma^{2}-\beta^{2})}(\bm{q})\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}]\Bigg].

Using this formula, we recalls the algorithm for calculating the cross sections.
The ”erxternal fixed” parameters that define the nature of the interaction are the
weak Fermi constant
G_{F}
, the (common) nucleon mass
m
, and
two sets of effective proton and neutron weak interaction constants,
\alpha_{p/n},\beta_{p/n},\gamma_{p/n},\delta_{p/n}
.
The ”external static” parameters are the mass
m_{A}
of the target nucleus and the mass
m_{\chi}
of the lepton.
The ”external dynamic” parameters are the initial kinetic energy

T_{0}
(or momentum
\bm{k}_{\chi}
) of the lepton incident on the nucleus at rest
and the recoil energy of this nucleus
T_{A}
.
Furthermore, the scattering angle
\theta
is defined in terms of them
using expression (
47
),
and the other quantities
E_{p}
,
E_{\chi}
,
\bm{k}
, etc. are defined by means of
formulas (
4.2
).

4.4
Massive (anti)neutrinos and
{V\!\mp\!A}
interaction

Consider an important case of the scattering of the massive neutral
\chi
lepton on the nucleus
due to the ”standard”
V\!\mp\!A
weak interaction.

Since calculation
Bednyakov:2021pgs
of the scalar products was carried out when the eigenstates of the
\chi
-lepton spin were the states of its helicity (projections of the spin onto the direction of its momentum),
the neutrino (whose helicity is negative) corresponds to the cross sections with the ”
--
”-indices of the
\chi
lepton, i.e., coherent
\dfrac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{coh}}}{dT_{A}}
and incoherent
\dfrac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{inc}}}{dT_{A}}
cross sections.
It is only due to the non-zero mass
m_{\chi}
that the processes with
\chi
-lepton spin flip whose cross sections are given in the second line of formulas (
4.3
) are possible.

First, from general formulas (
4.3
)
we get expressions for the cross sections for coherent scattering on the nuclei of massive neutrinos from the Standard Model.
Let us use formulas
(
62
),
corresponding to the assumption that the nuclear proton and neutron form factors are equal
(
61
).
For the
V\!-\!A
weak interaction of neutrinos with nucleons,
the latter have the following effective coupling constants
from (
49
):

\alpha_{f}=+g_{V}^{f},\quad\beta_{f}=-g_{A}^{f},\quad\gamma_{f}=-g_{V}^{f},\quad\delta_{f}=+g_{A}^{f}.

(87)

Then the
\bm{q}
-independent notations
(
63
)
are simplified and take the form

\displaystyle G_{\alpha}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{V}^{p}A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}A_{n}\equiv G_{V}(A),\qquad\qquad G_{\gamma}(A)=-(g_{V}^{p}A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}A_{n})=-G_{V}(A),

\displaystyle G_{\delta}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{A}^{p}A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}A_{n}\equiv G_{A}(A),\qquad\qquad G_{\beta}(A)=-(g_{A}^{p}A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}A_{n})=-G_{A}(A);

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\alpha}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{V}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}\Delta A_{n}\equiv\Delta G_{V}(A),\quad\Delta G_{\gamma}(A)=-(g_{V}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}\Delta A_{n})=-\Delta G_{V}(A),

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\delta}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{A}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}\Delta A_{n}\equiv\Delta G_{A}(A),\quad\Delta G_{\beta}(A)=-(g_{A}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}\Delta A_{n})=-\Delta G_{A}(A).

With their help one obtains
from (
62
)
a general set of
\chi A
cross sections of coherent scattering
of the massive
\chi
lepton due to
V
–
A
interaction in the form

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)[f_{\alpha+}(\theta)\pm f_{\gamma+}(\theta)]+G_{A}(A)[f_{\delta-}(\theta)\pm f_{\beta-}(\theta)]

\displaystyle\qquad+\Delta G_{V}(A)[f_{\gamma-}(\theta)\pm f_{\alpha-}(\theta)]+\Delta G_{A}(A)[f_{\beta+}(\theta)\pm f_{\delta+}(\theta)]\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}m^{2}_{\chi}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)(m+\lambda^{2}_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})-G_{A}(A)\lambda^{2}_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

(88)

\displaystyle\qquad-\Delta G_{V}(A)\lambda_{+}\lambda_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)[\lambda_{+}\lambda_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\mp m]\Big|^{2}.

For negative initial helicity (
s=-1
, upper right index) of the
massive
\chi
lepton corresponding to the neutrino with
V
–
A
weak interaction,
formulas (
4.4
) take
the explicit form

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})(|\bm{k}|+E_{\chi})^{2}\Big|G_{V}(A)(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+|\bm{k}|)+\Delta G_{V}(A)|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+G_{A}(A)(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)(E_{p}+|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{+-}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}_{\chi}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})-\Delta G_{V}(A)|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

(89)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-G_{A}(A)(E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)(|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m)\Big|^{2}.

It is taken into account that for sums of
f
-form factors from (
4.4
) one has

\displaystyle f_{\alpha+}(\theta)+f_{\gamma+}(\theta)\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!(|\bm{k}|+E_{\chi})(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+|\bm{k}|),\ f_{\alpha-}(\theta)+f_{\gamma-}(\theta)=(|\bm{k}|+E_{\chi})|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle f_{\beta-}(\theta)+f_{\delta-}(\theta)\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!(|\bm{k}|+E_{\chi})(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},\ f_{\beta+}(\theta)+f_{\delta+}(\theta)=(|\bm{k}|+E_{\chi})(E_{p}+|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}).

Recall that in the invariant variables the following relations hold

\displaystyle E_{\chi/p}=\dfrac{s\pm m^{2}_{\chi}\mp m^{2}}{2\sqrt{s}},

\displaystyle|\bm{k}|=\dfrac{\lambda(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}{2\sqrt{s}},\quad[\bm{k}^{l}_{\chi}]^{2}=\dfrac{\lambda^{2}(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}{4m^{2}},\ \text{~giving}

\displaystyle 4^{2}c_{A}(E_{\chi}+|\bm{k}|)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{(E_{\chi}+|\bm{k}|)^{2}}{m^{2}|\bm{k}^{l}_{\chi}|^{2}}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{\varkappa(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}{s},\text{~~~where}

\displaystyle\varkappa(s,m^{2}_{\chi})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\varkappa(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})=\Big[1+\dfrac{s+m^{2}_{\chi}-m^{2}}{\lambda(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}\Big]^{2},\quad\text{as well}

(90)

\displaystyle 4^{2}c_{A}m_{\chi}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{m_{\chi}^{2}}{m^{2}|\bm{k}^{l}_{\chi}|^{2}}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{\pi}\dfrac{m_{\chi}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}.

Then for the massive
\chi
lepton incident on the nucleus with negative initial helicity
and interacting with nucleons via the
V
–
A
weak current, coherent
\chi A
cross sections
(
4.4
)
can be
finally
written in the form

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\varkappa(s,m^{2}_{\chi})\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)\Big(\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Delta G_{V}(A)\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+G_{A}(A)\frac{(E_{p}-m)}{\sqrt{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)\Big(\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}+\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big)\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{+-}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{\pi}\dfrac{sm_{\chi}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}(s,m^{2}_{\chi})}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)\Big(\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big)-\Delta G_{V}(A)\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-G_{A}(A)\frac{(E_{p}-m)}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)\Big(\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)\Big|^{2}.

Formulas (
4.4
)
give the main result for the coherent scattering cross section of the massive neutrino on the nucleus
due to the weak
V
–
A
interaction
10
10

10

If one wants to ”restore” dependence on the nucleon type in the nuclear form factors
F^{2}(\bm{q})
,
one should ”hide them back” into the parameters
G_{V}(A,\bm{q})
and
G_{A}(A,\bm{q})
.
. In invariant variables, the coherent sections of the
V
–
A
weak interaction of the massive neutrino incident
on the
spinless
(
\Delta A_{f}=0
) nucleus take the form

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[1-\frac{T_{A}}{T^{\max}_{A}}\Big]\Big[\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\Big]F^{2}(\bm{q})\varkappa(s,m^{2}_{\chi})\Big\{G_{A}(A)\frac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}-m^{2}_{\chi}}{2s}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

(92)

\displaystyle\qquad+G_{V}(A)\Big[\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}+\frac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}-m^{2}_{\chi}}{2s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\dfrac{\lambda(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}{2{s}}\Big]\Big\}^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{+-}_{\text{coh,V-A}}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big[\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\Big]\dfrac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}\dfrac{s}{m^{2}}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big\{-G_{A}(A)\frac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}-m^{2}_{\chi}}{2s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+G_{V}(A)\Big[\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}+\frac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}-m^{2}_{\chi}}{2s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]\Big\}^{2},\quad\text{where}\quad\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\simeq\frac{T_{A}}{T^{\max}_{A}}.

There are two comments about these formulas.
First, in the case of a massless Standard Model neutrino, the second formula must vanish, which is clearly seen due to its proportionality to the square of the neutrino mass
m^{2}_{\chi}
.
Second, for
m_{\chi}=0
one gets
\lambda(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi}=0)=(s-m^{2})
.
Hence it follows that

\varkappa(s,m^{2}_{\chi}=0)=4\text{~~~and~~~}\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}+\dfrac{(s-m^{2})}{2{s}}+\frac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}}{2s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}=1-\dfrac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}}{2{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}.

As a result, the first formula goes into the expression

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{coh}}}{dT_{A}}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{\pi}\Big[1-\frac{T_{A}}{T^{\max}_{A}}\Big]F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big[G_{V}(A)\big[1-\dfrac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}}{2{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\big]+G_{A}(A)\frac{(\sqrt{s}-m)^{2}}{2s}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]^{2},\hskip 18.49988pt

(93)

which coincides with the corresponding formula from
Bednyakov:2021ppn
.
In other words, formula
(
92
)
for the coherent scattering cross section of massive neutrinos on (spinless) nuclei
at
m_{\chi}\to 0
goes exactly into the well-known formula for the massless neutrino
Bednyakov:2021ppn
.

About the second formula
(
92
),
note the following.
This cross section (with lepton spin flip) has an appreciable value only for ”appreciable”
mass of this lepton; moreover, the angular distribution proportional to
\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}
is very different from the case without the lepton spin flip (the first formula with
\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}
distribution).
The latter means that the lepton spin flip (due to the conservation of the total angular momentum of the system) is possible only if the spin-changing lepton flies out of the interaction zone almost exactly in the opposite direction.
On the other hand, for (extremely) small momenta
|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}\ll m^{2}_{\chi}
of the lepton incident on the nucleus,
firstly, the momentum
\bm{q}
transferred to the nucleus is also very small, and hence the nuclear form factor

F^{2}(\bm{q})\simeq 1
plays no role; second,
\sqrt{s}\simeq m+m_{\chi}
, and then the coherent

\chi A
cross section with lepton spin flip takes the form

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{+-}_{\text{coh}}(\chi A)}{dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}G^{2}_{V}(A).

(94)

It can be seen that if
\dfrac{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}{m^{2}_{\chi}}\equiv\Theta\ll 1
,
then the coherent
\chi A
cross section with spin flip is enhanced not only by the
A^{2}
nuclear factor
hidden in the parameter
G^{2}_{V}(A)
, but also by a sufficiently large ”particle flux” factor
\Theta^{-1}
.
This argument is not invalidated if
|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}\simeq m^{2}_{\chi}\ll m^{2}
, then

s\simeq m^{2}
and the second formula
(
92
)
goes into expression
(
94
).

Note further that formula for the
V
–
A
cross sections
(
4.4
),
while maintaining the positive helicity of the
\chi
lepton (right superscript
+
),
includes the following combinations of nucleon
f
-form factors
from (
4.3
):

\displaystyle f_{\alpha+}(\theta)-f_{\gamma+}(\theta)\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}-|\bm{k}|),\ \ f_{\gamma-}(\theta)-f_{\alpha-}(\theta)=(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle f_{\delta-}(\theta)-f_{\beta-}(\theta)\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},\hskip 9.24994ptf_{\beta+}(\theta)-f_{\delta+}(\theta)=(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)(|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}-E_{p}).

All these quantities for ”
++
”-helicity are proportional to the difference
(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)
, and not to the sum
(E_{\chi}+|\bm{k}|)
, as for ”
--
”-helicity.
This means that
V
–
A
-couplings from
(
87
)
strongly ”suppress” (proportionally to
(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)^{2}
) the coherent cross sections of interaction
between the nucleus and a massive antineutrino-analogue,
and upon passing to the massless limit, these cross sections simply vanish.
Thus, for the ”
++
”-helicity of the massive analogue of the antineutrino, it is necessary to set the coupling constants according to the
V
+
A
character of the weak interaction corresponding specifically to the antineutrino.
Indeed, in the case of antineutrinos (due to the
V
+
A
current), the nucleon weak coupling constants
look like

\alpha^{V+A}_{f}\equiv\alpha^{\bar{\nu}}_{f}=+g_{V}^{f},\quad\beta^{\bar{\nu}}_{f}=-g_{A}^{f},\quad\gamma^{\bar{\nu}}_{f}=+g_{V}^{f},\quad\delta^{\bar{\nu}}_{f}=-g_{A}^{f}.

(95)

Then
\bm{q}
-independent factors (
63
) for
V
+
A
cross sections are

\displaystyle G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\alpha}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{V}^{p}A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}A_{n}\equiv G_{V}(A),\qquad\Delta G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\alpha}(A)=g_{V}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}\Delta A_{n}\equiv\Delta G_{V}(A),

\displaystyle G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\gamma}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{V}^{p}A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}A_{n}=G_{V}(A),\qquad\Delta G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\gamma}(A)=g_{V}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{V}^{n}\Delta A_{n}=\Delta G_{V}(A),

\displaystyle G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\delta}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(g_{A}^{p}A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}A_{n})\equiv-G_{A}(A),\quad\Delta G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\beta}(A)=-(g_{A}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}\Delta A_{n})=-\Delta G_{A}(A),

\displaystyle G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\beta}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(g_{A}^{p}A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}A_{n})=-G_{A}(A),\quad\Delta G^{\bar{\nu}}_{\delta}(A)=-(g_{A}^{p}\Delta A_{p}+g_{A}^{n}\Delta A_{n})\equiv-\Delta G_{A}(A).

After substituting these factors into formulas
(
4.3
),
expressions are obtained for the coherent cross sections of the massive lepton scattering on the nucleus
due to the
V
+
A
interaction

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh,V+A}}(\bm{q})}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)[f_{\alpha+}(\theta)\mp f_{\gamma+}(\theta)]-G_{A}(A)[f_{\delta-}(\theta)\mp f_{\beta-}(\theta)]

(96)

\displaystyle\qquad-\Delta G_{V}(A)[f_{\gamma-}(\theta)\mp f_{\alpha-}(\theta)]+\Delta G_{A}(A)[f_{\beta+}(\theta)\mp f_{\delta+}(\theta)]\Big|^{2};

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh,V+A}}(\bm{q})}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}m^{2}_{\chi}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)(m+\lambda^{2}_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})+G_{A}(A)\lambda^{2}_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm\Delta G_{A}(A)m+

\displaystyle\qquad+[\Delta G_{V}(A)+\Delta G_{A}(A)]\lambda_{+}\lambda_{-}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big|^{2}.

It can be seen that these formulas ”correspond” to antineutrinos (helicity is positive
for a weak
V
+
A
-current).
The ”
++
”-helicity states correspond to the sum
f_{\alpha+}(\theta)+f_{\gamma+}(\theta)\propto(E_{\chi}+|\bm{k}|)
, which does not vanish in the massless and nonrelativistic cases when
E_{\chi}\simeq|\bm{k}|
.
If a
\chi
particle with the ”
--
”-helicity ”wants” to interact via the weak
V
+
A
-current (like a neutrino),
then its coherent cross section
(first formula (
96
)) will be suppressed
by the factor
(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)^{2}
and vanishes when
E_{\chi}\simeq|\bm{k}|
.

Therefore, coherent
\chi A
cross sections for the ”antineutrino-like” (
++
,
-+
and
V\!+\!A
indices)
and ”neutrino-like” (
--
,
+-
and
V\!-\!A
indices)
interactions of the massive
\chi
lepton with the nucleus have the form

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(|\bm{k}|+E_{\chi})^{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+|\bm{k}|)\pm G_{A}(A)(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

(97)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\pm\Delta G_{V}(A)|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)(E_{p}+|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\Big|^{2};

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\pm\mp}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}_{\chi}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}F^{2}(\bm{q})\Big|G_{V}(A)(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\mp G_{A}(A)(E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\mp\Delta G_{V}(A)|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\Delta G_{A}(A)(m+|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\Big|^{2}.

It should be emphasized that each of formulas
(
97
)
contains only two possible expressions,
when only superscripts or only subscripts are
simultaneously
taken for the cross section
(spin indices are
\mp\mp
)
and for the current (
\mp
).

Note that, as previousely for neutrinos, at (extremely) small momenta
|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}\ll m^{2}_{\chi}

of the (right-hand) lepton incident on the nucleus, one has the
F^{2}(\bm{q})\simeq 1
and
\sqrt{s}\simeq m+m_{\chi}
,

|\bm{k}|\simeq 0
,
E_{p}\simeq m
,
hence the coherent
\chi A
cross section with spin flip of the (left- and right-handed) lepton
are the same
:

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\pm\mp}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A}}(\bm{q})}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{m^{2}_{\chi}}{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big|G_{V}(A)+\Delta G_{A}(A)\Big|^{2}.

(98)

Thus, if
\dfrac{|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}{m^{2}_{\chi}}\equiv\Theta\ll 1
,
the coherent
\chi A
cross sections with spin flip (of both left- and right-handed massive lepton)
are enhanced not only by the influence of the nucleus, but also by a sufficiently large ”particle flux” factor
\Theta^{-1}
.
These observations
look new
and probably are important for registration of the relic massive (anti)neutrinos
with extremely low energy (
5\div 6\times 10^{-4}~
eV).
Despite the rather enhanced cross section, the old question of

what we are going to measure
remains open.

From general formula
(
60
)
one can obtain the fully averaged coherent
\chi A
cross section
on the spinless nucleus

due to the
V\mp A
interaction, i.e. when the nucleon coupling constants
(
87
)
and (
95
) are

\alpha_{V\mp A}=+g_{V},\ \beta_{V\mp A}=-g_{A},\ \gamma_{V\mp A}=\mp g_{V},\ \delta_{V\mp A}=\pm g_{A}.

(99)

The
\bm{q}
-dependent effective coupling constants from
(
4.3
)
take the ”
V\!\mp\!A
” form

\displaystyle G_{\alpha_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\sum(+g_{V}^{f})A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})\equiv+G_{V}(A,\bm{q}),\ \ G_{\gamma_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})=\sum(\mp g_{V}^{f})A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})\equiv\mp G_{V}(A,\bm{q}),\hskip 18.49988pt

\displaystyle G_{\beta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\sum(-g_{A}^{f})A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})=-G_{A}(A,\bm{q}),\ \ G_{\delta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})=\sum(\pm g_{A}^{f})A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})\equiv\pm G_{A}(A,\bm{q}).\hskip 18.49988pt

Similar to (
4.4
),
spin-dependent factors are

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\alpha_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum(+g_{V}^{f})\Delta A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})\equiv+\Delta G_{V}(A,\bm{q}),

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\gamma_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum(\mp g_{V}^{f})\Delta A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})\equiv\mp\Delta G_{V}(A,\bm{q}),

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\beta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum(-g_{A}^{f})\Delta A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})=-\Delta G_{A}(A,\bm{q}),

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\delta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum(\pm g_{A}^{f})\Delta A_{f}F_{f}(\bm{q})\equiv\pm\Delta G_{A}(A,\bm{q}).

Substitution of (
4.4
) in
(
60
)
gives
the fully averaged coherent
V\!\mp\!A
cross section
for the
scattering of the massive lepton on the nucleus
without spin
(
\Delta A_{f}=0
):

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big[G_{V}(A,\bm{q})E_{\chi}\big(E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{|\bm{k}|^{2}}{E_{\chi}}\big)\pm G_{A}(A,\bm{q})E_{\chi}(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]^{2}

(101)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big[G_{V}(A,\bm{q})|\bm{k}|(E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{\chi})\pm G_{A}(A,\bm{q})|\bm{k}|(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle m_{\chi}^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big[G_{V}(A,\bm{q})(E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\mp G_{A}(A,\bm{q})(E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big]^{2}.

This coherent
V\!\mp\!A
cross section can be expanded in
\bm{q}
-dependent effective constants

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi m^{2}|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}\big[G_{V}^{2}(A,\bm{q})T_{V}(\theta)+G_{A}^{2}(A,\bm{q})T_{A}(\theta)\pm 2G_{V}(A,\bm{q})G_{A}(A,\bm{q})T_{M}(\theta)\big],\hskip 9.24994pt

(102)

where the coefficients of this expansion are

\displaystyle T_{A}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(E_{p}-m)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(2|\bm{k}|^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m_{\chi}^{2}),

\displaystyle T_{M}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}|\bm{k}|^{2}(E_{\chi}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}),

\displaystyle T_{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})^{2}(2|\bm{k}|^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m_{\chi}^{2})+\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}|\bm{k}|^{2}\big[m_{\chi}^{2}+2|\bm{k}|^{2}+4E_{\chi}(E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\big].

Formula
(
102
)
gives the coherent
\chi A
cross section (
\Delta A_{f}=0
) for
V\!\mp\!A
variants of the weak interaction of the massive

\chi
lepton with the nucleus, fully summed over the helicities of the final lepton and averaged over the helicities of the initial lepton.

To verify the calculations, let us consider a transition of the general formulas of coherent scattering to the limit
of
V\!\mp\!A
currents.
To this end, we substitute

V\!\mp\!A
parameters from
(
4.4
)
into the general formulas for the cross sections
(
4.3
)
in the following form:

\displaystyle G_{\alpha_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[+G_{V}(\bm{q})],\qquad G_{\gamma_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})=[\bm{\mp}G_{V}(\bm{q})],

\displaystyle G_{\beta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\bm{-}G_{A}(\bm{q})],\qquad G_{\delta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})=[\bm{\pm}G_{A}(\bm{q})];

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\alpha_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\bm{+}\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})],\quad\Delta G_{\gamma_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})=[\bm{\mp}\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})],

\displaystyle\Delta G_{\beta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\bm{-}\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})],\quad\Delta G_{\delta_{\mp}}(A,\bm{q})=[\bm{\pm}\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})].

The result is
all
coherent
\chi A
cross sections in the
V\!\mp\!A
approximation

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh},\bm{V\!-\!A}}}{\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|(f_{\alpha+}(\theta)\pm f_{\gamma+}(\theta))G_{V}(\bm{q})+(f_{\delta-}(\theta)\pm f_{\beta-}(\theta))G_{A}(\bm{q})

\displaystyle+(f_{\gamma-}(\theta)\pm f_{\alpha-}(\theta))\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})+(f_{\beta+}(\theta)\pm f_{\delta+}(\theta))\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh},\bm{V\!-\!A}}}{\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|G_{V}(\bm{q})\hat{f}_{\alpha}(\theta)-G_{A}(\bm{q})\hat{f}_{\delta-}(\theta)-\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})\hat{f}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)+(\hat{f}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)\mp\hat{f}_{\delta+}(\theta))\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})\Big|^{2};

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh},\bm{V\!+\!A}}}{\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|(f_{\alpha+}(\theta)\mp f_{\gamma+}(\theta))G_{V}(\bm{q})\bm{-}(f_{\delta-}(\theta)\mp f_{\beta-}(\theta))G_{A}(\bm{q})

\displaystyle-(f_{\gamma-}(\theta)\mp f_{\alpha-}(\theta))\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})+(f_{\beta+}(\theta)\mp f_{\delta+}(\theta))\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh},\bm{V\!+\!A}}}{\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|G_{V}(\bm{q})\hat{f}_{\alpha}(\theta)+G_{A}(\bm{q})\hat{f}_{\delta-}(\theta)+\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})\hat{f}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)\bm{+}(\hat{f}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)\pm\hat{f}_{\delta+}(\theta))\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})\Big|^{2}.

Since for
f
-form factors from
(
4.4
)
for
V\!\mp\!A
we have

\displaystyle f_{\alpha+}(\theta)\pm f_{\gamma+}(\theta)\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm|\bm{k}|),\ \ f_{\gamma-}(\theta)\pm f_{\alpha-}(\theta)=(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle f_{\delta-}(\theta)\pm f_{\beta-}(\theta)\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},\hskip 9.24994ptf_{\beta+}(\theta)\pm f_{\delta+}(\theta)=(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)(|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm E_{p}),

and according to (
4.3
)

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{\alpha}(\theta)=m_{\chi}(m+(E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}),\ \hat{f}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)=m_{\chi}|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},\ \hat{f}_{\delta-}(\theta)=m_{\chi}(E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},\ \hat{f}_{\delta+}(\theta)=m_{\chi}m,

then
the complete set
of coherent
\chi A
cross sections for
V\!-\!A
- and
V\!+\!A
-weak currents is

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh},{V\!-\!A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\Big|(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm|\bm{k}|)G_{V}(\bm{q})

\displaystyle+(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}G_{A}(\bm{q})+|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})+(|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm E_{p})\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh},{V\!+\!A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(E_{\chi}\mp|\bm{k}|)^{2}\Big|(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\mp|\bm{k}|)G_{V}(\bm{q})-(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}G_{A}(\bm{q})

(103)

\displaystyle-|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})+(|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\mp E_{p})\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})\Big|^{2};

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh},{V\!-\!A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}m_{\chi}^{2}\Big|m[G_{V}(\bm{q})\mp\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})]+[G_{V}(\bm{q})-G_{A}(\bm{q})](E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle+[\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})-\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})]|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh},{V\!+\!A}}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}m_{\chi}^{2}\Big|m[G_{V}(\bm{q})\pm\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})]+[G_{V}(\bm{q})+G_{A}(\bm{q})](E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle+[\Delta G_{V}(\bm{q})+\Delta G_{A}(\bm{q})]|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big|^{2}.

The expressions for the cross sections for the spinless nucleus (
\Delta A_{f}=0
) can be obtained
from (
4.4
)
by discarding terms proportional to
\Delta G
.
They can be used, for example, to directly check the expansion
of the fully averaged coherent

V\mp A
cross sections on the spinless nucleus
(
102
)
in effective
\bm{q}
-dependent coupling constants.
Indeed, at
\Delta A_{f}=0
the
V\!-\!A
interaction has the following expansions
in effective
\bm{q}
-dependent coupling constants:

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh,V-A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\Big[G_{V}^{2}(\bm{q})(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}+G_{A}^{2}(\bm{q})(E_{p}-m)^{2}\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle+2G_{V}(\bm{q})G_{A}(\bm{q})(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm|\bm{k}|)(E_{p}-m)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big],

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\pm}_{\text{coh,V-A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}m_{\chi}^{2}\Big[G_{V}^{2}(\bm{q})(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})^{2}+G_{A}^{2}(\bm{q})(E_{p}-m)^{2}\cos^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle-2G_{V}(\bm{q})G_{A}(\bm{q})(m\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})(E_{p}-m)\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big].

Then the averaged coherent ”
V\!-\!A
” cross section, which is the sum (divided by 2)
of the above-mentioned four terms can be written as

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{\text{coh,V-A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{s^{\prime}s=\pm}\frac{d\sigma^{s^{\prime}s}_{\text{coh,V-A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}\equiv G_{V}^{2}(\bm{q})C_{V}(\theta)+G_{A}^{2}(\bm{q})C_{A}(\theta)+2G_{V}(\bm{q})G_{A}(\bm{q})C_{M}(\theta).

For the averaged coherent
V\!+\!A
cross section, exactly the same expansion is obtained
but with the minus sign in front of the interference term (see formula
(
102
)).
As a result, with (
38
),
the averaged coherent
V\!\mp\!A
cross section on the spinless nucleus takes the form

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A},0}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\Big\{G_{V}^{2}(\bm{q})\dfrac{C_{V}(\theta)}{|\bm{k}|^{2}s}+G_{A}^{2}(\bm{q})\dfrac{C_{A}(\theta)}{|\bm{k}|^{2}s}\pm 2G_{V}(\bm{q})G_{A}(\bm{q})\dfrac{C_{M}(\theta)}{|\bm{k}|^{2}s}\Big\}.\qquad

(104)

Direct calculations give expressions for the parameters
C_{A}(\theta)
,
C_{V}(\theta)
and
C_{M}(\theta)

coinciding with
T(\theta)
-parameters from
(
102
),
although they were obtained in completely different ways.

Finally, in the
massless

\chi
-lepton limit, when

m_{\chi}=0,\ \dfrac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}=\dfrac{s+m^{2}}{2{s}},\ \dfrac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}=\dfrac{E_{\chi}}{\sqrt{s}}=\dfrac{s-m^{2}}{2{s}},

C_{V,A,M}
-parameters from
(
104
)
contain only one term each, which corresponds to the conservation of helicity of the
neutrino (
V\!-\!A
) or antineutrino (
V\!+\!A
). They are

\displaystyle\dfrac{C_{A}(\theta)}{|\bm{k}|^{2}s}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\hat{C}_{A}(\theta)_{0}=\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big(1-\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)^{4}\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle\dfrac{C_{M}(\theta)}{|\bm{k}|^{2}s}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\hat{C}_{M}(\theta)_{0}=\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big(1-\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big[2-\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big(1-\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)^{2}\Big],

\displaystyle\dfrac{C_{V}(\theta)}{|\bm{k}|^{2}s}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\hat{C}_{V}(\theta)_{0}=\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big[2-\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big(1-\frac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)^{2}\Big]^{2}.

Then for the spinless nucleus and
m_{\chi}=0
, the total coherent cross section
(
104
)
can be written as

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A},00}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{8\pi}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big\{G_{V}^{2}(\bm{q})\hat{C}_{V}(\theta)_{0}+G_{A}^{2}(\bm{q})\hat{C}_{A}(\theta)_{0}\pm 2G_{V}(\bm{q})G_{A}(\bm{q})\hat{C}_{M}(\theta)_{0}\Big\}.\qquad

(105)

In fact, for the massless lepton, the ”completely averaged”
coherent cross section contains only one term
from each formula (
97
).
For
V\!-\!A
current, it is
half
the cross section with ”
--
”-helicity (”
-
”-superscripts),
for
V\!+\!A
current, it is
half
the cross section with ”
++
”-helicity (”
+
”-subscripts) from (
97
):

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\sigma^{\text{total}}_{\text{coh,V$\mp$A},00}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}\equiv\dfrac{1}{2}\sum_{s^{\prime}s}\frac{d\sigma^{s^{\prime}s}_{\text{coh}}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{coh},{V\!\mp\!A},00}}{g_{\text{c}}dT_{A}}=\frac{1}{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{\pi}\Big[G_{V}(\bm{q}){T_{V,0}(\theta)}\pm G_{A}(\bm{q}){T_{A,0}}\Big]^{2}.

Parameters
T_{V,0}(\theta)
and
T_{A,0}(\theta)
for
m_{\chi}=0
take the form

\displaystyle T_{V,0}(\theta)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\dfrac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\dfrac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\to 1-\Big(1-\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)^{2}\frac{\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}}{2},

\displaystyle T_{A,0}(\theta)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Big(\dfrac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}-\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\to\Big(1-\dfrac{m}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)^{2}\frac{\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}}{2}.

The parameters
T_{V,0}
and
T_{A,0}
completely correspond to the above expression
(
93
)
for the coherent cross section of the interaction of the massless neutrino with the nucleus from

Bednyakov:2021ppn
.
The latter means that the averaged coherent cross section of the
V\!-\!A

interaction of the massless left-handed helicity lepton with the spinless nucleus
(
105
)
is exactly half of the purely ”neutrino cross section”
(
93
).

Incoherent
{\chi A}
-sections for
\bm{V\!\mp\!A}
weak currents.

Recall that the nucleon constants in the case of
V\!\mp\!A
weak currents have the form
(
99
)

\alpha_{V\!\mp\!A}\equiv\alpha_{\mp}=+g_{V},\ \beta_{\mp}=-g_{A},\ \gamma_{\mp}=\mp g_{V},\ \delta_{\mp}=\pm g_{A}.

Then the general expansions of the incoherent
\chi A
cross sections
(
65
)
go into the following ”
V\!\mp\!A
” expressions for
scattering without lepton spin flip
:

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V-A}}}{g_{\text{i}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{c}_{A}}{2}\sum_{f=p,n}A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[g_{V}^{2}\widetilde{V}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)+g_{A}^{2}\widetilde{A}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)+g_{V}g_{A}\widetilde{M}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)

(106)

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\big\{g_{V}g_{A}\widetilde{M}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)-g_{V}^{2}\widetilde{V}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)-g_{A}^{2}\widetilde{A}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)\big\}\Big],

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V+A}}}{g_{\text{i}}\hat{c}_{A}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{c}_{A}}{2}\sum_{f=p,n}A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[g_{V}^{2}\widetilde{V}^{+}_{\mp}(\theta)+g_{A}^{2}\widetilde{A}^{+}_{\mp}(\theta)-g_{V}g_{A}\widetilde{M}^{+}_{\mp}(\theta)

\displaystyle\qquad\mp\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\big\{g_{V}g_{A}\widetilde{M}^{-}_{\mp}(\theta)+g_{V}^{2}\widetilde{V}^{-}_{\mp}(\theta)+g_{A}^{2}\widetilde{A}^{-}_{\mp}(\theta)\big\}\Big].

In (
106
), the following notations are introduced:

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{V}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{F^{-}_{\alpha^{2}}(\theta)}{2}+\frac{F^{-}_{\gamma^{2}}(\theta)}{2}\pm\frac{F^{-}_{\alpha\gamma}(\theta)}{2}=-2(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}(|\bm{k}|\pm E_{p}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{A}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{F^{-}_{\beta^{2}}(\theta)}{2}+\frac{F^{-}_{\delta^{2}}(\theta)}{2}\pm\frac{F^{-}_{\beta\delta}(\theta)}{2}=-2(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})E_{p}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{M}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)}{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{F^{-}_{\alpha\delta}(\theta)}{4}+\frac{F^{-}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)}{4}\pm\Big[\frac{F^{-}_{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{4}+\frac{F^{-}_{\gamma\delta}(\theta)}{4}\Big]=(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\big[(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm 2E_{p}|\bm{k}|\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}\big],

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{M}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)}{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{F^{+}_{\alpha\delta}(\theta)}{4}+\frac{F^{+}_{\beta\gamma}(\theta)}{4}\pm\Big[\frac{F^{+}_{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{4}+\frac{F^{+}_{\gamma\delta}(\theta)}{4}\Big]=2(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}(|\bm{k}|\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm E_{p})|\bm{k}|\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

(107)

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{A}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{F^{+}_{\beta^{2}}(\theta)}{2}+\frac{F^{+}_{\delta^{2}}(\theta)}{2}\pm\frac{F^{+}_{\beta\delta}(\theta)}{2}=(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}[(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+2(E_{p}^{2}-|\bm{k}|^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}],

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{V}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{F^{+}_{\alpha^{2}}(\theta)}{2}+\frac{F^{+}_{\gamma^{2}}(\theta)}{2}\pm\frac{F^{+}_{\alpha\gamma}(\theta)}{2}=(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\big[2|\bm{k}|^{2}\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}+(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\big].

Form-factor expressions
(
4.4
)
completely determine the incoherent
\chi A
cross sections
without spin flip
of the massive
\chi
lepton
(
106
)
in the case of
V\!\mp\!A
interaction.
It can be seen that all parameters with the subscript ”
+
” contain the common factor

(E_{\chi}+|\bm{k}|)^{2}
, and all parameters with thee subscript ”
-
” are proportional to the
common factor
(E_{\chi}-|\bm{k}|)^{2}
, which makes the cross section vanish
in the relativistic limit when
E_{\chi}\simeq|\bm{k}|
.
Since

2^{4}c_{A}(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}{m^{2}|\bm{k}_{\chi}|^{2}}=\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{\varkappa_{\pm}(s,m^{2}_{\chi})}{s},

where, by analogy with
(
90
),
the following notation is introduced:

\varkappa(s,m^{2}_{\chi})_{\pm}\equiv\varkappa(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})_{\pm}=\Big[1\pm\dfrac{s+m^{2}_{\chi}-m^{2}}{\lambda(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}\Big]^{2},

(108)

the set of incoherent ”
V\!\mp\!A
” cross sections without spin flip of the massive
\chi
lepton
can
finally
be written as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V-A}}}{g_{\text{i}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\varkappa_{\pm}(s,m^{2}_{\chi})\sum A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[g_{V}^{2}{V}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)+g_{A}^{2}{A}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)+g_{V}g_{A}{M}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)

(109)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\pm\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\big\{g_{V}g_{A}{M}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)-g_{V}^{2}{V}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)-g_{A}^{2}{A}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)\big\}\Big],

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V+A}}}{g_{\text{i}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\varkappa_{\mp}(s,m^{2}_{\chi})\sum A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[g_{V}^{2}{V}^{+}_{\mp}(\theta)+g_{A}^{2}{A}^{+}_{\mp}(\theta)-g_{V}g_{A}{M}^{+}_{\mp}(\theta)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\mp\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\big\{g_{V}g_{A}{M}^{-}_{\mp}(\theta)+g_{V}^{2}{V}^{-}_{\mp}(\theta)+g_{A}^{2}{A}^{-}_{\mp}(\theta)\big\}\Big].

In formulas (
109
)
parameters (
4.4
)
are redefined as dimensionless quantities

\displaystyle{V}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)\equiv\frac{\widetilde{V}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2s(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\Big(\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\pm\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle{A}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)=\frac{\widetilde{A}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2s(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\Big(\frac{E_{p}^{2}}{s}\pm\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle{M}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)\equiv\frac{\widetilde{M}^{-}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2s(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}{s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm 4\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle{M}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)\equiv\frac{\widetilde{M}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2s(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\Big(\frac{|\bm{k}|}{\sqrt{s}}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm\frac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}\Big)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

(110)

\displaystyle{A}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)\equiv\frac{\widetilde{A}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2s(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}{s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+2\Big(\frac{E_{p}^{2}}{s}-\frac{|\bm{k}|^{2}}{s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\Big)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2},

\displaystyle{V}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)\equiv\frac{\widetilde{V}^{+}_{\pm}(\theta)}{2s(E_{\chi}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{|\bm{k}|^{2}}{s}\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{(E_{p}\pm|\bm{k}|)^{2}}{s}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}.

Formulas (
109
)
give the incoherent cross sections for massive
\chi
-lepton scattering on the nucleus
without spin flip

simultaneously due to
V
–
A
and
V
+
A
currents.
Note that in the first case the cross section is for the lepton with positive helicity (analogue antineutrino),
and in the second case it is for the lepton with negative helicity (analogue neutrino).

If one
substitutes parameters from
(
4.4
)
in formulas (
109
)
and introduces another notation

\xi=\dfrac{|\bm{k}|}{E_{p}}=\dfrac{\lambda(s,m^{2},m^{2}_{\chi})}{s-m^{2}_{\chi}+m^{2}},

then one can obtain ”more explicit” expressions for ”
V\!\mp\!A
” incoherent
\chi A
cross sections
in the following form:

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V-A}}}{g_{\text{i}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{E_{p}^{2}}{{s}}\varkappa_{\pm}(s,m^{2}_{\chi})\sum A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[(g_{V}^{2}+g_{A}^{2})(1\pm\xi)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+2(g_{V}\xi\pm g_{A})^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

(111)

\displaystyle-2(g_{V}-g_{A})^{2}\xi^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\big\{2g_{V}g_{A}(1\pm\xi)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+2(g_{V}\xi\pm g_{A})^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\pm 2(g_{V}-g_{A})^{2}\xi\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}-g_{V}^{2}\xi\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(\xi\pm\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\big\}\Big],

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V+A}}}{g_{\text{i}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{E_{p}^{2}}{{s}}\varkappa_{\mp}(s,m^{2}_{\chi})\sum A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[(g_{V}^{2}+g_{A}^{2})(1\mp\xi)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+2\big(g_{V}\xi\pm g_{A}\big)^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle-2(g_{V}+g_{A})^{2}\xi^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\mp\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\big\{2g_{V}g_{A}(1\mp\xi)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}-2(g_{V}\xi\pm g_{A})^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\pm 2(g_{V}+g_{A})^{2}\xi\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+g_{V}^{2}\xi\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(\xi\mp\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\big\}\Big].

This completes the discussion of presentations of the incoherent cross sections for
the weak
V\!\mp\!A
interaction
of the massive
\chi
lepton with the nucleus without lepton spin flip.

In the massless limit, when

\displaystyle m_{\chi}=0,\quad\dfrac{E_{p}}{\sqrt{s}}=\dfrac{s+m^{2}}{2{s}},\quad\xi=\dfrac{s-m^{2}}{s+m^{2}},\quad\varkappa(s,0)_{+}=4,\quad\varkappa(s,0)_{-}=0,

cross sections (

)
for leptons with ”wrong helicity”
become zero

\frac{d\sigma^{++}_{\text{inc,V-A},0}}{dT_{A}}\propto\varkappa_{-}(s,0)=0,\qquad\frac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{inc,V+A},0}}{dT_{A}}\propto\varkappa_{-}(s,0)=0.

For massless leptons with ”correct helicity”, formulas
(
111
) turn into

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V$\mp$A},0}}{g_{\text{i}}dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{4\pi}\dfrac{4E_{p}^{2}}{{s}}\sum A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Big[(g_{V}^{2}+g_{A}^{2})(1+\xi)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+2(g_{V}\xi\pm g_{A})^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

(112)

\displaystyle-2(g_{V}\mp g_{A})^{2}\xi^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}+\dfrac{\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\Big\{2g_{V}g_{A}(1+\xi)^{2}\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}

\displaystyle\pm 2(g_{V}\xi\pm g_{A})^{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\pm 2(g_{V}\mp g_{A})^{2}\xi\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\mp g_{V}^{2}\xi\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(\xi+\cos^{2}\frac{\theta}{2})\Big\}\Big].

This formula corresponds to the incoherent scattering of neutrinos and antineutrinos on the nucleus,
which was discussed in
Bednyakov:2021ppn
.
It is a direct consequence of the general expansion in chiral constants
(
109
), whose
”
V
–
A

11
11

11

Here, the

and

and

.
matching the following expression from

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{--}_{\text{inc,V-A},0}}{dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{2\pi}\sum_{f=p,n}A_{f}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}\Bigg[g_{A}^{2}\Big[2\Big(1-\dfrac{ys}{s-m^{2}}\Big)+y^{2}+y\dfrac{4m^{2}}{s-m^{2}}\Big]+2g_{V}g_{A}y(2-y)

(113)

\displaystyle+g_{V}^{2}\Big[2\Big(1-\dfrac{ys}{s-m^{2}}\Big)+y^{2}\Big]+\dfrac{2\Delta A_{f}}{A_{f}}\Big\{g_{V}^{2}y\Big(1-\frac{y}{2}\frac{s+m^{2}}{s-m^{2}}\Big)+g_{A}^{2}y\Big(1-\frac{y}{2}\Big)\frac{s+m^{2}}{s-m^{2}}

\displaystyle+g_{V}g_{A}\Big[2\Big(1-\frac{ys}{s-m^{2}}\Big)+y^{2}\frac{s+m^{2}}{s-m^{2}}\Big]\Big\}\Bigg].

Finally, let us see what the incoherent
\chi A
cross section
(

)

approximation
passes into when

\displaystyle(\alpha\mp\gamma)_{V\!\mp\!A}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2g_{V},\quad(\beta\mp\delta)_{V\!\mp\!A}=-2g_{A},\quad(\alpha\mp\gamma)_{V\!\pm\!A}=(\delta\mp\beta)_{V\!\pm\!A}=0.

Substituting these relations into formula (

),

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V$\mp$A},00}}{dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{G^{2}_{F}m_{A}}{2\pi}\sum_{f=p,n}\widehat{F}_{f}^{2}(\bm{q})A_{f}\Big[g_{A}^{2}\Big(2+y^{2}+2y\dfrac{2m^{2}-s}{s-m^{2}}\Big)\pm 2g_{V}g_{A}y(2-{y})

\displaystyle+g_{V}^{2}\Big(2+y^{2}-\dfrac{2ys}{s-m^{2}}\Big)\Big];

\displaystyle\frac{d\sigma^{\mp\mp}_{\text{inc,V$\pm$A},00}}{dT_{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0.

As expected, the first formula, which is
the incoherent cross section for scattering of the
massless neutrino (with negative helicity) and massless antineutrino (with positive helicity) on the nucleus,
coincides (in its spinless part) with the ”neutrino formula” (

).
The second formula, which is the cross section of the massless neutrino scattering via the

current
and massless antineutrino scattering via the
